import time
import numpy as np

def _safe_const_candidate(prefer_len=1, cap=1000000):
    n = prefer_len
    if not isinstance(n, int) or n <= 0:
        n = 1
    if n > cap:
       n=1
    return [1.0] * n

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(1.0, float(budget_s) - 5.0)
    n = 1
    try:
       h = height_sequence_1
       if hasattr(h, 'size'):
          n = int(h.size)
       else:
          n = len(h)
    except Exception:
       n = 1
    if n < 1: n = 1
    # For memory and exact validation, cap maybe 200000? Parent 6962
    if n > 100000:
       # return small candidate; still valid and optimal
       cand = [1.0]
    else:
       cand = [1.0] * n
    # exact verify if feasible
    try:
       sc = float(evaluate_sequence(cand))
    except Exception:
       sc = float('inf')
       cand = [1.0]
    # if score invalid, [1.0]
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
       cand = [1.0]
       try: sc = float(evaluate_sequence(cand)); except...
    # Since theorem says 2 is lower bound; no improvement possible. Return immediately.
    return cand
```

Let's craft robust function:
```
import time
import numpy as np

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        total = max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    except Exception:
        total = 1.0
    deadline = start + max(0.5, total - 5.0)

    # The supplied evaluator maps every finite nonnegative c to
    # 2*n*max((c*c)) / sum(c)^2.  By Cauchy max >= b0 >= sum^2/n,
    # so score >= 2, equality for any positive constant vector.
    # Thus a constant vector is globally optimal.

    parent_len = 1
    try:
        h = height_sequence_1
        if hasattr(h, "size"):
            parent_len = int(h.size)
        else:
            parent_len = len(h)
    except Exception:
        parent_len = 1
    if parent_len < 1 or not np.isfinite(parent_len):
        parent_len = 1

    # Use parent length when it is affordable; otherwise use minimal length.
    # exact evaluate_sequence for n ones costs O(n^2); cap to keep it fast.
    MAX_N_EXACT = 10000
    if parent_len <= MAX_N_EXACT:
        candidate = [1.0] * parent_len
    else:
        candidate = [1.0]

    def _score(seq):
        try:
            return float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
        except Exception:
            return float('inf')

    score = _score(candidate)
    # In case something unexpected happens, minimal constant is a valid fallback.
    fallback = [1.0]
    fb_score = _score(fallback)
    if not np.isfinite(score) or (np.isfinite(fb_score) and fb_score < score):
        candidate = fallback
        score = fb_score

    # Optional: if parent itself scores even better (could only happen due to
    # floating roundoff or a different runtime evaluator), keep it. Do not
    # spend substantial time because constant is already the analytic optimum
    # for the printed objective.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0 and parent_len <= MAX_N_EXACT:
        try:
            parent = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if parent.size == parent_len and np.all(np.isfinite(parent)):
                par_seq = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in parent]
                par_score = _score(par_seq)
                if np.isfinite(par_score) and par_score < score - 1e-15:
                    candidate = par_seq
                    score = par_score
        except Exception:
            pass

    # final attempt if budget says score still target high? No need.
    return candidate
```

Let's decide final code:
```
import time
import numpy as np

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        total = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        total = 1300.0
    # Reserve a few seconds for return/validation in cases where we decide to optimize/heavy validate.
    deadline = start + max(0.25, total - 2.0)

    def _time_left():
        return max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())

    # Determine an affordable length. The exact optimum is any positive constant vector.
    length = 1
    try:
        h = height_sequence_1
        if hasattr(h, "size"):
            length = int(h.size)
        else:
            length = len(h)
    except Exception:
        length = 1
    if length < 1:
        length = 1

    # Use the parent length when it is small enough to validate exactly and the budget allows.
    # For the provided parent this is 6962, so this branch is taken.
    affordable = (length <= 10000) and (total >= 10.0)
    if not affordable:
        length = 1

    candidate = [1.0] * length

    def _score(seq):
        try:
            val = evaluate_sequence(seq)
        except Exception:
            return float("inf")
        try:
            val = float(val)
        except Exception:
            return float("inf")
        return val if np.isfinite(val) else float("inf")

    score = _score(candidate)
    fallback = [1.0]
    fallback_score = inf
    if _time_left() > 0.05:
        fallback_score = _score(fallback)
        if not np.isfinite(fallback_score):
            fallback_score = 2.0 # theoretical? But we shouldn't use surrogate. However fallback valid. We can keep.
        if (not np.isfinite(score)) or (np.isfinite(fallback_score) and fallback_score < score):
            candidate = fallback
            score = fallback_score

    # The objective's analytic lower bound is 2; if the runtime evaluator reports a
    # significantly lower finite score, that means the evaluator differs from the printed
    # formula. In that rare case, do a tiny amount of safe black-box improvement from any
    # better parent.
    if np.isfinite(score) and score < 2.0 - 1e-8 and _time_left() > 5.0:
        try:
            parent = None
            try:
                parent = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            except Exception:
                parent = None
            if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
                par = np.clip(parent, 0.0, 1000.0)
                if np.all(np.isfinite(par)) and par.sum() >= 0.01:
                    par_score = _score(par.tolist())
                    if np.isfinite(par_score) and par_score < score:
                        candidate = par.tolist()
                        score = par_score
                    # Maybe try a few uniformly scaled versions? Score invariant, no.
                    # Try a coarse coordinate scaling if time? Could skip.
        except Exception:
            pass
    # If budget remains, and if we are still at the theoretical bound 2, there is no
    # descent direction under printed evaluator. For any runtime evaluator that allows <2,
    # the parent/black-box parent has already been considered; return best valid.
    # Ensure finite output.
    try:
        candidate = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in candidate]
    except Exception:
        candidate = [1.0]
    if not candidate:
        candidate = [1.0]
    return candidate
```

def _active_weights(B, S, exact_score, beta):
    L = B.size
    if exact_score > 0 and S > 0:
        B_est = exact_score*S*S/(2.0*n)
    else:
        B_est = B[1:].max() if L>1 else 0
    tol = max(1e-8, 1e-4*max(1.0, B_est))
    active = np.abs(B - B_est) <= tol
    # Also include lags with B slightly above but not too high? If B[0] high, no.
    cnt = active.sum()
    if cnt == 0:
        # fallback: exclude 0 and last if B_est < B[0]*(0.95), top K interior
        exclude0 = B[0] > B_est*1.01 if B_est>0 else True
        # anyway choose top K with B <= B_est*1.1 if possible else all
        K = min(L, max(64, L//20))
        # Use np.argpartition on B to get top K
        top = np.argpartition(B, -K)[-K:] if L > K else np.arange(L)
        # If B_est finite, drop those much larger than B_est? maybe not
        active = np.zeros(L,bool); active[top]=True
    # If active includes 0 but B_est < B[0]*0.99, remove 0? Probably.
    if B_est > 0 and B[0] > B_est*1.01 and active[0]:
        active[0]=False; active[n-?]? no.
    if not active.any():
        active = np.ones(L,bool)
    # limit active size to KMAX by top B among active
    KMAX = max(64, min(4096, L//5))
    if active.sum() > KMAX:
        idx=np.where(active)[0]
        vals=B[idx]
        # top KMAX
        part=np.argpartition(vals, -KMAX)[-KMAX:]
        new=np.zeros(L,bool); new[idx[part]]=True; active=new
    # set p proportional to exp((B-Bmax)/tau)? We'll compute later. Return active
```

Greedy algorithm vectorized:
```
cnt = np.zeros(2*n-1, dtype=np.int32)
sel = np.empty(m, dtype=np.int32)
k=0
for x in random_subset:
    if k>=m: break
    s2 = 2*x
    # self add 1; distinct add 2. Since x not in sel, no existing y=x.
    if cnt[s2] >= R: continue
    if k>0:
        sums = x + sel[:k]
        # Need ensure cnt[sums] +2 <= R. If any violation, reject.
        # Also if duplicate sums within same x? sums distinct because sel distinct. Good.
        # But if any sums includes s2? x+sel =2x only if sel contains x, not.
        if np.any(cnt[sums] >= R-1): continue  # cnt+2 >R
        cnt[sums] += 2
    cnt[s2] +=1
    sel[k]=x; k+=1
```
Need sums max x+max sel ≤2n-2; cnt length okay. dtype int32 enough. This ensures max ordered count ≤R if no initial counts >R. Note that when updating cnt[sums], some sums may appear twice? For fixed x, sums x+y distinct as y distinct. Good.

def get_weights(B, beta):
    Bmax = B.max()
    # use weights proportional exp(- (Bmax - B)/tau)
    x = (B - Bmax) / tau  # <=0
    # clip to -50
    w = np.exp(np.maximum(x, -50))
    wsum = w.sum()
    if wsum==0: uniform top
    return w/wsum
```

```
import numpy as np, time

def _next_pow2(m): return 1 << (m-1).bit_length() if m>1 else 1

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    start=time.monotonic()
    total=max(1.0,float(budget_s))
    deadline=start+max(1.0,total-15.0)
    # get parent length/array
    try: parent = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except: parent = np.ones(6962)
    parent = np.clip(parent,0,1000)
    if parent.size ==0 or not finite or sum<0.01: parent=np.ones(6962)
    n = parent.size
    # If n too large? FFT okay up to maybe 50000. But parent 6962. If n>50000, use subset? Use 6962? We can still maybe not. For robust, if n>50000: return [1.0]? But parent length known.
    convN = next_pow2(2*n-1)
    corrN = next_pow2(3*n-2)

    def conv_self(a):
       A=np.fft.rfft(a, convN)
       B=np.fft.irfft(A*A, convN)
       return B[:2*n-1]
    def score_f(a, B=None):
       if B is None: B=conv_self(a)
       S=a.sum()
       if S<=0: return inf
       return 2.0*n*B.max()/(S*S)
    def corr(w,a): ...
    def normalize(a, target=n):
       a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
       s=a.sum()
       if s < 0.01: return None
       a = a * (target/s)
       # optional cap
       if a.max() > 1000.0:
          a=np.minimum(a,1000.0)
          s=a.sum()
          if s<0.01: return None
       return a
```

```
def run_optimize(a, beta0, beta1, min_improve=1e-8, stuck_limit=200, max_iters, phase_deadline):
   a = normalize(a)
   B=conv_self(a); sc=surrogate_score(a,B)
   best_local=a; best_sc=sc
   v=None; it=0; stuck=0
   while it<max_iters and time.monotonic()<phase_deadline:
       it+=1
       B=conv_self(a); S=sum? n; sc=...
       # weights
       Bmax=B.max()
       beta = beta0 + (beta1-beta0)*min(1,it/max(1,max_iters//2))? Better use time: beta = beta0 * (beta1/beta0)**(it/max_iters) if beta1>beta0
       temp = max(1e-8, Bmax/beta)
       x = -(Bmax-B)/temp
       w = np.exp(np.clip(x,-50,0))
       ssum=w.sum()
       if not finite or ssum==0: w uniform top
       else w/=ssum
       c = corr(w,a)
       d = 2*a + c
       d = d - d.mean()
       dmax = np.max(np.abs(d))
       if dmax < 1e-12: perturb; continue
       dir = d/dmax
       if v is not None: dir = 0.7*dir + 0.3*v? Keep v as normalized? Need line search with dir.
       # line search
       base_alphas = [0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2]
       best_trial=None; best_trial_sc=sc
       for alpha in base_alphas:
           cand = a - alpha*dir
           cand = normalize(cand)
           if cand is None: continue
           Bc=conv_self(cand)
           sc_c=2*n*Bc.max()/(cand.sum()**2)
           if sc_c < best_trial_sc - 1e-12:
               best_trial_sc=sc_c; best_trial=cand; best_alpha=alpha
       if best_trial is not None and best_trial_sc < best_local_sc - 1e-9:
           a=best_trial; best_local=...
           v=dir (or update)
           stuck=0
           if exact time: evaluate
       else:
           # maybe try in negative? If no improvement, try smaller alpha or perturb
           if stuck < stuck_limit:
              # reduce dir using momentum? 
              v = None? 
           else:
              perturb; reset beta?
           stuck+=1
   return best_local, best_local_sc
```

def weights_from_B(B, beta):
    L=B.size
    Bmax=B.max()
    if not finite or Bmax<=0: uniform
    beta_eff = max(1e-8, beta)
    temp = Bmax / beta_eff
    # If temp too small relative to Bmax? okay.
    x = -(Bmax - B)/temp
    # clip min -700 maybe
    w = np.exp(np.clip(x, -700, 0))
    s = w.sum()
    if s <=0 or not finite:
       # top few
       idx=np.argsort(B.ravel())[-min(100,L):]
       w=np.zeros(L); w[idx]=1/wcount
    else:
       w /= s
    return w
```

Define helpers using closures.

```
def _conv(a):
   A=np.fft.rfft(a, conv_len)
   B=np.fft.irfft(A*A, conv_len)
   return B[:2*n-1]
def _score(a, B=None):
   if B is None: B=_conv(a)
   S=a.sum()
   if S <= 0: return inf
   return 2.0*n*float(np.max(B))/(S*S)
def _corr(w,a):
   wr = w[::-1]
   W=np.fft.rfft(wr, corr_len)
   A=np.fft.rfft(a, corr_len)
   v=np.fft.irfft(W*A, corr_len)
   return v[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
```
Need `corr_len` at least 3n-2. For n=6962, 32768. Good.

Normalize:
```
def _normalize(a):
   a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
   a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
   s=a.sum()
   if s < 1e-12: return None
   a *= n/s
   if a.max() > 1000.0:
      a *= 999.0/a.max()
   if not finite: return None
   return a
```
If after cap scaling sum <0.01? If all tiny? a was scaled to n before cap; if max huge, scale down, sum could be <0.01 only if n small huge max. Fallback.

Weights:
```
def _weights(B, beta, top_frac, mix_top):
   L=B.shape[0]; Bmax=float(B.max())
   if Bmax<=0: return np.ones(L)/L
   beta=max(1e-6,beta)
   temp=Bmax/max(beta,1e-6)
   x=-(Bmax-B)/temp
   w=np.exp(np.clip(x,-700.0,0.0))
   s=w.sum()
   if not finite or s==0:
      w=np.ones(L)/L
   else: w/=s
   if mix_top is not None and mix_top>0:
      K=max(32, int(L*top_frac))
      if K<L:
        idx=np.argpartition(B, -K)[-K:]
      else: idx=np.arange(L)
        top=np.zeros(L); top[idx]=1.0/K; top/=top.sum()
      w=(1-mix_top)*w + mix_top*top
      w/=w.sum()
   return w
```
For top uniform, if K small (active constraints) could be center interior and derivative constant; but mix <1, all softmax background helps. Use top_frac=0.05 (K700), mix_top increase from 0.0 to 0.3. Early none.

Optimize:
```
def _optimize(a, phase_seconds, beta0, beta1, top_frac, mix0, mix1, exact_every):
   a=_normalize(a); if None return a, inf
   # Initial exact? caller.
   B=_conv(a); sc=_score(a,B)
   best=a.copy(); best_sc=sc
   local_best=sc; last_improve=0; v=None; it=0
   phase_deadline=start+phase_seconds? better global deadline chunk.
   while time.monotonic()<chunk_deadline:
      it+=1
      B=_conv(a); S=a.sum(); sc=2n maxB/S^2
      if sc < local_best - 1e-9: local_best=sc; last_improve=it; if sc < best_sc: best=a.copy(); best_sc=sc
      # beta linearly over chunk or based on it? Use phase progress:
      elapsed = time.monotonic()-phase_start
      frac = min(1, elapsed/phase_seconds)
      beta = beta0 + (beta1-beta0)*frac
      mix = mix0 + (mix1-mix0)*frac
      w=_weights(B,beta,top_frac,mix)
      c=_corr(w,a)
      d=c - d.mean()
      dmax=np.max(np.abs(d))
      if dmax < 1e-14: perturb...
      dir=d/dmax
      if v is not None:
         nd=np.linalg.norm(dir); vdir = v / np.linalg.norm(v) ...
         dir = 0.7*dir + 0.3*vdir; normalize?
      # line search
      current_sc=sc
      best_trial=None; best_trial_sc=current_sc
      for alpha in [0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35]:
          trial=a - alpha*dir
          trial=_normalize(trial)
          if trial is None: continue
          Bt=_conv(trial); st=_score(trial,Bt)
          if st < best_trial_sc - 1e-12:
             best_trial=trial; best_trial_sc=st
      if best_trial is not None:
          a=best_trial; v=dir
          if a score exact? add to checkpoint
          last_improve=it
      else:
          # if no improve, maybe try negative direction? No, maybe step too large? Try very small 0.005, 0.01? We already. If fail, perturb.
          if it - last_improve > 80:
              # perturb
              amp=0.05 + 0.01*min(5,(//80))
              a=_normalize(a + amp*rng.normal(size=n))
              v=None
              local_best = _score(a,_conv(a)); last_improve=it
   return best, best_sc
```
But line search with alphas from small to large: If small gives improvement but larger worse, pick best among all. Good. If no positive alpha improves, but negative direction (a + alpha*dir) might? Maybe if many no improve, test + for one alpha. But likely - correct. We can have fallback: if no improvement, try `a + 0.02*dir`; if improve, use it and set `dir=-dir` next? Simpler: accept no, perturb.

Let's define `evaluate_exact_seq(arr)`:
```
def _exact(arr):
   arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000)
   if arr.size==0 or not finite or arr.sum()<0.01: return inf
   seq=[float(x) for x in arr.tolist()]
   try: val=float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
   except: return inf
   return val if finite else inf
```
This does normalization? Evaluator clips. We can pass arr. For exact score, if arr has sum <0.01 inf. Our arrays sum ~n.

Perturbation details:
```
def _perturb(a, amp):
   # zero mean random to preserve scale
   r=rng.normal(size=n)
   r -= r.mean()
   return _normalize(a + amp*r)
```

Now, code for `_optimize` needs access to `rng`, `n`, helpers, `deadline_global`? We'll pass `phase_deadline` and `phase_start`.

Pseudo:
```
def _optimize(a, phase_deadline, beta0, beta1, top_frac, mix0, mix1):
    a0 = _normalize(a)
    if a0 is None: return None, inf
    phase_start = time.monotonic()
    phase_dur = max(1.0, phase_deadline - phase_start)
    a = a0
    B = _conv(a)
    local_best = _score(a,B)
    best = a.copy(); best_sc = local_best
    v = np.zeros(n)
    use_v = False
    last = 0; it=0; beta=beta0; mix=mix0
    stuck=0
    while time.monotonic() < phase_deadline:
       it +=1
       if time.monotonic() > phase_deadline - 0.5: break
       B = _conv(a)
       sc = _score(a, B)
       if sc < local_best - 1e-10:
          local_best=sc; best=a.copy(); best_sc=sc; last=it; stuck=0
       # beta/mix target
       frac = min(1.0, (time.monotonic()-phase_start)/phase_dur)
       beta_target = beta0 + (beta1-beta0)*frac
       mix_target = mix0 + (mix1-mix0)*frac
       if beta < beta_target: beta += 0.05*(beta_target-beta)
       elif beta > beta_target: beta = beta_target
       mix = mix_target
       w = _weights(B, beta, top_frac, mix)
       c = _corr(w,a)
       d = c - c.mean()
       dn = np.max(np.abs(d))
       if not np.isfinite(dn) or dn < 1e-14:
           # perturb
           amp=0.1+0.05*min(5, stuck*?)
           a=_normalize(a+amp*(rng.normal(size=n)-?))
           v=np.zeros(n); use_v=False; beta=max(beta0,beta*0.5); last=it; it? continue
       dir = d/dn
       if use_v:
           vnorm=np.linalg.norm(v)
           if vnorm>1e-12:
              dir = 0.7*dir + 0.3*(v/vnorm)
              dir = dir / (np.linalg.norm(dir)+1e-300) # but this changes scale; normalized max maybe
              # We normalize by max abs later
       # line search
       best_trial=None; best_trial_sc=sc
       # Use current sc as baseline from B
       for alpha in (0.005,0.01,0.03,0.1,0.2):
           # check time
           tri = a - alpha*dir
           tri = _normalize(tri)
           if tri is None: continue
           Bt = _conv(tri)
           st = _score(tri, Bt)
           if st < best_trial_sc - 1e-12:
              best_trial=tri; best_trial_sc=st
       if best_trial is not None:
           a = best_trial
           v = dir; use_v=True
       else:
           # try negative? maybe one tiny
           tri = a + 0.02*dir
           tri=_normalize(tri)
           if tri is not None:
              st=_score(tri,_conv(tri))
              if st < sc - 1e-10:
                  a=tri; v=-dir; use_v=True
       if it - last > 80:
           stuck +=1
           amp = 0.05 + 0.03*min(5,stuck)
           r = rng.normal(size=n); r-=r.mean()
           a = _normalize(a + amp*r)
           v=np.zeros(n); use_v=False
           beta = max(beta0, beta*0.5)
           # update local_best should reset? set sc from conv? We can set local_best = min(local_best, _score(a,_conv(a))) but if perturb worse, keep best. last=it
   return best, best_sc
```

   try:
      if isinstance(arr, list): seq=arr
      else:
         a=np.asarray(arr,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
         if a.size==0 or not finite or a.sum()<0.01: return inf

Phase deadlines: Use initial `work_start = time.monotonic()` after exact parent. `work_total = max(0.0, work_end - work_start)`. If work_total <=0, skip optimize.

Build alt lists before phases. Computing surrogate for inits may take time; if time > work_end, skip.

Initialize `best_surr_seq` and score:
```
if parent is not None: best_surr_seq=parent.copy(); best_surr_sc=_score(parent, _conv(parent))
else: best_surr_seq=np.ones(n); best_surr_sc=2.0
```
But `_score(parent, None)` does conv; already maybe not. Use function.

Saved add initial.

Phase loop:
```
phases = []
phases.append({'arr': parent if parent is not None else best_surr_seq, 'frac':0.5, ...})
if alts: phases.append({'arr': alts[0], 'frac':0.2,...}) else none
phases.append({'arr': 'perturb', 'frac':0.15,...})
phases.append({'arr': 'best', 'frac':0.15,...})
remaining = work_total
for ph in phases:
   if time >= work_end: break
   dur = ph['frac']*work_total
   deadline = min(work_end, time.monotonic()+dur)
   if deadline - time.monotonic() < 3: continue
   arr = ph['arr'] or perturb/best
   ...
```
Using fixed fractions of initial work_total. If a phase skipped, time lost. okay. We can use cumulative deadlines from start for determinism:
```
cum=0
deadlines=[0.5,0.7,0.85,1.0]
```
If skipped, next still uses time.

For phase3 perturbed best: `base = best_surr_seq.copy(); r=...; arr=_normalize(base + 0.1*base.mean()*r)`. If None, use base.

After each phase, if ret not None:
```
if ret_sc < best_surr_sc: best_surr_seq=ret; best_surr_sc=ret_sc
save_seq(ret, ret_sc)
```
Should we exact evaluate ret immediately to update best_exact? Could but final. If saved, final.

At final, evaluate saved candidates and best_surr. Ensure `best_exact_seq` maybe [1.0] if no parent exact due to time? But we did parent exact early. Good.

Potential duplicate candidate in saved by surrogate score; no issue.

Now, if exact evaluation at final of parent length n takes longer than reserve due to many saved, we break before hard_end. Ensure at least final best_exact_seq is valid. If best_exact_seq is [1.0] or parent already exact. Good.

Let's include final validation: before return, if best_exact_seq is not finite, set [1.0]. Convert to list. Do not call evaluate_sequence again unless time and maybe to verify? We already have score; but rules say verify final with exact. We have verified best_exact_seq at time it was set (parent/fallback). If best_exact_seq from final exact evaluation, verified. Good.

If best_exact_seq is from `_exact(parent)` but parent array later transformed? no.

`_exact` when evaluating saved arr may check sum and clip. If arr sum <0.01, inf. Fine.

Now, let's write final code. Ensure use of `np` imported. Avoid f-strings? okay. Use `float("inf")`.

One more consideration: The evaluator's `np.convolve` for sequence length maybe if values are numpy floats? Our final list Python floats. Good.

Let's produce code.
</think>

```python
import numpy as np
import time


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        total = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        total = 1300.0
    total = max(1.0, total)

    # Keep a hard stop to avoid being killed after exhausting time without returning.
    hard_end = t0 + max(0.5, total - 2.0)
    reserve = 30.0 if total > 100.0 else max(1.0, 0.2 * total)
    work_end = t0 + max(1.0, total - reserve)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # Use the supplied parent construction directly if available.
    try:
        par = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        par = np.ones(6962, dtype=np.float64)

    if par.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(par)):
        par = np.ones(6962, dtype=np.float64)

    # Dense exact validation is O(n^2); cap the working length for robustness.
    MAXN = 20000
    if par.size > MAXN:
        par = par[:MAXN].copy()

    n = int(par.size)
    if n < 1:
        return [1.0]

    # If the budget is tiny, avoid quadratic exact checks on a long parent.
    if total < 8.0 and n > 1000:
        return [1.0]

    par = np.clip(par, 0.0, 1000.0)

    def _pow2(m):
        return 1 << (max(1, int(m)) - 1).bit_length()

    conv_len = _pow2(2 * n - 1)
    corr_len = _pow2(3 * n - 2)

    def _conv_self(a):
        A = np.fft.rfft(a, conv_len)
        B = np.fft.irfft(A * A, conv_len)
        B = B[:2 * n - 1]
        B = np.maximum(B, 0.0)
        return B

    def _score(a, B=None):
        if B is None:
            B = _conv_self(a)
        s = float(np.sum(a))
        if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
            return float("inf")
        m = float(np.max(B))
        if not np.isfinite(m):
            return float("inf")
        return 2.0 * float(n) * m / (s * s)

    def _weights_uw(a, w):
        """c_j = sum_k w_k a_{k-j} for j=0..n-1."""
        wr = w[::-1]
        W = np.fft.rfft(wr, corr_len)
        A = np.fft.rfft(a, corr_len)
        v = np.fft.irfft(W * A, corr_len)
        return v[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]

    def _normalize(a):
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if a.size != n:
            a = a[:n]
        a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
        s = float(np.sum(a))
        if s <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(s):
            return None
        a = a * (float(n) / s)
        if np.max(a) > 1000.0:
            a = a * (999.0 / np.max(a))
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)) or float(np.sum(a)) < 0.01:
            return None
        return a

    def _exact_sequence(arr):
        try:
            if isinstance(arr, list):
                seq = [float(x) for x in arr]
            else:
                a = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
                if a.size == 0 or a.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
                    return float("inf")
                a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
                if float(np.sum(a)) < 0.01:
                    return float("inf")
                seq = a.tolist()
            v = float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
            if np.isfinite(v):
                return v
        except Exception:
            pass
        return float("inf")

    def _weights(B, beta, top_frac, top_mix):
        L = B.size
        Bmax = float(np.max(B))
        if not np.isfinite(Bmax) or Bmax <= 0.0:
            return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / L

        beta = max(1e-6, float(beta))
        temp = Bmax / beta
        x = -(Bmax - B) / temp
        w = np.exp(np.clip(x, -744.0, 0.0))
        sw = float(np.sum(w))
        if not np.isfinite(sw) or sw <= 0.0:
            w = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / L
        else:
            w = w / sw

        if top_mix is not None and top_mix > 1e-8:
            K = max(32, int(round(top_frac * L)))
            if K >= L:
                top = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / L
            else:
                idx = np.argpartition(B, -K)[-K:]
                top = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
                top[idx] = 1.0
                top /= top.sum()
            w = (1.0 - top_mix) * w + top_mix * top
            w /= float(np.sum(w))
        return w

    def _perturb(a, amp):
        r = rng.normal(size=n)
        r -= r.mean()
        sd = float(np.std(r))
        if sd > 1e-12:
            r /= sd
        scale = max(float(np.mean(a)), 1e-12)
        out = _normalize(a + amp * scale * r)
        return out

    def _optimize(a, phase_deadline, beta0, beta1, top_frac, mix0, mix1):
        a0 = _normalize(a)
        if a0 is None:
            return None, float("inf")

        phase_start = time.monotonic()
        dur = max(1.0, phase_deadline - phase_start)

        a = a0
        B = _conv_self(a)
        local_best = _score(a, B)
        best = a.copy()
        best_sc = local_best
        last_improve = 0
        it = 0
        beta = float(beta0)
        v = None

        alphas = (0.005, 0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.35)

        while time.monotonic() + 0.25 < phase_deadline:
            it += 1
            R = time.monotonic()

            B = _conv_self(a)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(B)):
                out = _perturb(a, 0.15)
                if out is not None:
                    a = out
                continue

            sc = _score(a, B)
            if sc < local_best - 1e-11:
                local_best = sc
                best = a.copy()
                best_sc = sc
                last_improve = it

            frac = min(1.0, (R - phase_start) / dur)
            beta_target = beta0 + (beta1 - beta0) * frac
            mix_target = mix0 + (mix1 - mix0) * frac
            beta = beta + 0.10 * (beta_target - beta)

            w = _weights(B, beta, top_frac, mix_target)
            c = _weights_uw(a, w)
            d = c - float(np.mean(c))
            dn = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))

            if not np.isfinite(dn) or dn < 1e-14:
                last_improve = it
                a2 = _perturb(a, 0.12)
                if a2 is not None:
                    a = a2
                v = None
                beta = max(beta0, 0.5 * beta)
                continue

            dirv = d / dn
            if v is not None and np.max(np.abs(v)) > 1e-14:
                vv = v / float(np.max(np.abs(v)))
                dirv = 0.72 * dirv + 0.28 * vv
                dn2 = float(np.max(np.abs(dirv)))
                if dn2 > 1e-14:
                    dirv = dirv / dn2

            scale = max(float(np.mean(a)), 1e-12)
            found = None
            found_sc = sc

            for alpha in alphas:
                if time.monotonic() + 0.1 > phase_deadline:
                    break
                trial = _normalize(a - alpha * scale * dirv)
                if trial is None:
                    continue
                Bt = _conv_self(trial)
                st = _score(trial, Bt)
                if st < found_sc - 1e-12:
                    found = trial
                    found_sc = st

            if found is None and it - last_improve > 40:
                # One cautious opposite step; sometimes helps when projection
                # makes the forward step non-descending due to clipping.
                trial = _normalize(a + 0.03 * scale * dirv)
                if trial is not None:
                    Bt = _conv_self(trial)
                    st = _score(trial, Bt)
                    if st < sc - 1e-10:
                        found = trial
                        found_sc = st
                        dirv = -dirv

            if found is not None:
                a = found
                local_best = min(local_best, found_sc)
                if found_sc < best_sc:
                    best = a.copy()
                    best_sc = found_sc
                last_improve = it
                v = dirv.copy()
            else:
                if v is not None:
                    v = 0.5 * v
                if it - last_improve > 90:
                    amp = 0.08 + 0.025 * min(6, (it - last_improve) // 90)
                    a2 = _perturb(a, amp)
                    if a2 is not None:
                        a = a2
                    v = None
                    beta = max(beta0, 0.55 * beta)
                    last_improve = it

            _ = R

        return best, best_sc

    # A minimal valid fallback with an exact score under the supplied evaluator.
    fallback = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    best_exact_seq = fallback.copy()
    best_exact_score = _exact_sequence(fallback.tolist())
    if not np.isfinite(best_exact_score):
        best_exact_score = 2.0

    parent = _normalize(par)
    parent_surr = 2.0
    if parent is not None and time.monotonic() < work_end - max(1.0, total * 0.02):
        pe = _exact_sequence(parent)
        if np.isfinite(pe) and pe < best_exact_score:
            best_exact_seq = parent.copy()
            best_exact_score = pe
        parent_surr = _score(parent)
    else:
        parent = _normalize(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    if parent is None:
        parent = _normalize(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
        parent_surr = 2.0

    def _save_candidate(arr, sc):
        pass  # replaced by direct saved list below

    saved = []

    def save_seq(arr, sc):
        if arr is None:
            return
        a = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if a.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
            return
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if float(np.sum(a)) < 0.01:
            return
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            sc = 1e100
        saved.append((float(sc), a.copy()))
        saved.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
        max_saved = 3 if n > 12000 else 6
        if len(saved) > max_saved:
            saved.pop()

    save_seq(parent, parent_surr)
    save_seq(best_exact_seq, best_exact_score)

    # Generate compact alternative initializations.
    alt_inits = []
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)

    def add_init(arr):
        arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if arr.size != n:
            arr = arr[:n]
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        out = _normalize(arr)
        if out is not None and float(np.sum(out)) >= 0.01:
            alt_inits.append(out)

    base = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    add_init(base)

    # Linear ramps.
    for amp in (0.35, 0.55, 0.75):
        add_init(base + amp * (2.0 * t - 1.0))

    # Parabolic / U-shaped.
    for amp in (0.35, 0.60):
        add_init(base + amp * ((2.0 * t - 1.0) ** 2 - 1.0 / 3.0))

    # Low-frequency sine/cosine perturbations.
    for f in (1, 2, 3, 5, 8):
        for amp in (0.30, 0.55):
            add_init(base + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * t))
            add_init(base + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * t))

    # Weak multisine random starts.
    for _ in range(3):
        arr = base.copy()
        for f in (1, 2, 3, 4):
            arr += (0.25 * rng.standard_normal()) * np.sin(
                2.0 * np.pi * f * t + rng.standard_normal()
            )
        add_init(arr)

    alt_cands = []
    for arr in alt_inits:
        if time.monotonic() >= work_end:
            break
        try:
            sc = _score(arr)
        except Exception:
            sc = float("inf")
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < 2.05:
            alt_cands.append((sc, arr))
    alt_cands.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
    alts = [a for _, a in alt_cands[:3]]

    best_surr_seq = parent.copy() if parent is not None else fallback.copy()
    best_surr_sc = parent_surr

    # Phases: parent basin, alternative starts, perturbed best, final polish.
    work_start = time.monotonic()
    work_total = max(0.0, work_end - work_start)

    if work_total > 5.0 and parent is not None:
        d0 = time.monotonic() + 0.50 * work_total
        d0 = min(d0, work_end)
        if d0 - time.monotonic() > 4.0:
            r, sc = _optimize(parent, d0, 3.0, 35.0, 0.10, 0.00, 0.25)
            if r is not None:
                save_seq(r, sc)
                if sc < best_surr_sc:
                    best_surr_seq = r.copy()
                    best_surr_sc = sc

    if work_total > 5.0 and alts and time.monotonic() < work_end:
        d1 = time.monotonic() + 0.22 * work_total
        d1 = min(d1, work_end)
        if d1 - time.monotonic() > 4.0:
            r, sc = _optimize(alts[0], d1, 5.0, 55.0, 0.08, 0.15, 0.40)
            if r is not None:
                save_seq(r, sc)
                if sc < best_surr_sc:
                    best_surr_seq = r.copy()
                    best_surr_sc = sc

    if work_total > 5.0 and time.monotonic() < work_end:
        base_arr = best_surr_seq
        if base_arr is not None and base_arr.size:
            pb = _perturb(base_arr, 0.10)
            if pb is None:
                pb = base_arr
        else:
            pb = None
        d2 = time.monotonic() + 0.14 * work_total
        d2 = min(d2, work_end)
        if pb is not None and d2 - time.monotonic() > 4.0:
            r, sc = _optimize(pb, d2, 8.0, 70.0, 0.05, 0.30, 0.50)
            if r is not None:
                save_seq(r, sc)
                if sc < best_surr_sc:
                    best_surr_seq = r.copy()
                    best_surr_sc = sc

    if work_total > 5.0 and time.monotonic() < work_end:
        d3 = work_end
        if d3 - time.monotonic() > 4.0:
            r, sc = _optimize(best_surr_seq, d3, 15.0, 80.0, 0.03, 0.40, 0.60)
            if r is not None:
                save_seq(r, sc)
                if sc < best_surr_sc:
                    best_surr_seq = r.copy()
                    best_surr_sc = sc

    # Final exact validation of the best surrogate candidates and the known
    # valid fallback.  The returned sequence is always one whose exact score was
    # checked by the supplied evaluator when possible.
    save_seq(best_surr_seq, best_surr_sc)
    save_seq(parent, parent_surr)
    save_seq(fallback, best_exact_score)

    seen = set()
    for sc, arr in saved:
        if time.monotonic() > hard_end - 2.5:
            break
        key = (arr.size, tuple(np.round(np.asarray(arr[: min(64, arr.size)], dtype=np.float64), 12).tolist()))
        if key in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(key)
        ex = _exact_sequence(arr)
        if np.isfinite(ex) and ex < best_exact_score:
            best_exact_score = ex
            best_exact_seq = arr.copy()

    # Ensure returned list is valid and finite.
    out = np.asarray(best_exact_seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if out.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(out)):
        out = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
        best_exact_score = _exact_sequence(out.tolist())
        if not np.isfinite(best_exact_score):
            out = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)

    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return [float(x) for x in out.tolist()]
```
